<div class="bg-base-100">
    <header class="border-base-content/20 bg-base-100 fixed top-0 z-10 w-full border-b py-0.25">

    <main class="h-screen">

        <div

        >
          <div class="bg-base-200 border-base-content/20 flex w-fit items-center gap-2.5 rounded-full border px-3 py-2">

          <h1

          >
            <span>Sizzling Summer Delights Effortless Recipes for Parties!</span>
            <svg
              width="223"
              height="12"
              viewBox="0 0 223 12"

  <script src="https://unpkg.com/tailwindcss-intersect@2.x.x/dist/observer.min.js" type="module"></script>

    <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/flyonui.js"></script>
    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    // Function to create random dots inside the entire viewport
    function createRandomDots(numDots) {
      const container = document.querySelector(".dot-container")
      const dotTemplate = document.getElementById("dot-template")

      // Remove any existing dots before generating new ones
      container.innerHTML = ""

      for (let i = 0; i < numDots; i++) {
        // Clone the dot
        const newDot = dotTemplate.cloneNode()

        // Generate random position within 3% to 95% range of viewport width and height
        const randomX = Math.random() * 92 + 3 // 3% to 95% range for X position
        const randomY = Math.random() * 92 + 3 // 3% to 95% range for Y position

        // Position the dot using percentages
        newDot.style.left = `${randomX}%`
        newDot.style.top = `${randomY}%`

        // Append the dot to the container
        container.appendChild(newDot)
      }
    }
    // Call the function to create 100 dots initially
    createRandomDots(30)

    // Fetaure marque
    const clone = document.querySelectorAll(".clone")
    clone.forEach(item => {
      const content = item.innerHTML
      item.innerHTML = content + content + content
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

    <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/flyonui.js"></script>
    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", function () {
      const header = document.getElementById("header")

      // Function to check and apply styles based on scroll position
      function checkScroll() {
        if (window.scrollY > 0) {
          header.classList.add("bg-base-100", "shadow-sm", "shadow-base-300/20")
        } else {
          header.classList.remove("bg-base-100", "shadow-sm", "shadow-base-300/20")
        }
      }

      // Check on page load
      checkScroll()

      // Listen for scroll events
      window.addEventListener("scroll", checkScroll)
    })
  </script>

  <script src="https://unpkg.com/tailwindcss-intersect@2.x.x/dist/observer.min.js" type="module"></script>

    <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/flyonui.js"></script>
    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", function () {
      const header = document.getElementById("header")

      // Function to check and apply styles based on scroll position
      function checkScroll() {
        if (window.scrollY > 0) {
          header.classList.add("bg-base-100", "shadow-sm", "shadow-base-300/20")
        } else {
          header.classList.remove("bg-base-100", "shadow-sm", "shadow-base-300/20")
        }
      }

      // Check on page load
      checkScroll()

      // Listen for scroll events
      window.addEventListener("scroll", checkScroll)

      // Fetaure marque
      const clone = document.querySelectorAll(".clone")
      clone.forEach(item => {
        const content = item.innerHTML
        item.innerHTML = content + content + content + content
      })
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", function () {
      const header = document.getElementById("header")

      // Function to check and apply styles based on scroll position
      function checkScroll() {
        if (window.scrollY > 0) {
          header.classList.add("bg-base-100", "shadow-sm", "shadow-base-300/20")
        } else {
          header.classList.remove("bg-base-100", "shadow-sm", "shadow-base-300/20")
        }
      }

      // Check on page load
      checkScroll()

      // Listen for scroll events
      window.addEventListener("scroll", checkScroll)
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    const clone = document.querySelectorAll(".clone")
    clone.forEach(item => {
      const content = item.innerHTML
      item.innerHTML = content + content
    })
  </script>

    <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/flyonui.js"></script>
    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", function () {
      const header = document.getElementById("header")

      window.addEventListener("scroll", function () {
        if (window.scrollY > 0) {
          header.classList.add("bg-base-100", "shadow-md", "shadow-base-300/20")
        } else {
          header.classList.remove("bg-base-100", "shadow-md", "shadow-base-300/20")
        }
      })

      const card = document.getElementById("card3d")

      // Variables for tracking the mouse position
      let rect, centerX, centerY

      const handleMouseMove = e => {
        // Get the dimensions of the card
        rect = card.getBoundingClientRect()
        // Calculate the center of the card
        centerX = rect.left + rect.width / 2
        centerY = rect.top + rect.height / 2

        // Calculate the mouse position relative to the center of the card
        const mouseX = e.clientX - centerX
        const mouseY = e.clientY - centerY

        // Calculate the rotation values based on mouse position
        // Limiting the rotation to a reasonable range
        const rotateY = mouseX * 0.015
        const rotateX = -mouseY * 0.015

        // Apply the 3D transformations directly to the card
        card.style.transform = `perspective(1000px) rotateX(${rotateX}deg) rotateY(${rotateY}deg) scale3d(1.05, 1.05, 1.05)`
        card.style.boxShadow = `0 15px 35px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2)`
      }

      card.addEventListener("mouseenter", () => {
        card.style.transition = "transform 0.2s ease, box-shadow 0.2s ease"
      })

      card.addEventListener("mousemove", handleMouseMove)

      card.addEventListener("mouseleave", () => {
        // Reset the transforms when mouse leaves
        card.style.transform = "perspective(1000px) rotateX(0) rotateY(0) scale3d(1, 1, 1)"
        card.style.boxShadow = "none"
        card.style.transition = "transform 0.5s ease, box-shadow 0.5s ease"
      })
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

                  <h1 class="text-base-content jehtf leading-[1.15] fl9z1 lfitq max-md:text-3xl">
                    2 Mexican Grilled Cheese Burgers + Fries
                  </h1>
                  <p class="text-base-content/80">
                    Double the flavor with our irresistible 2 Mexican Grilled Cheese Burgers, each packed with a bold
                    fusion of spicy Mexican herbs, juicy grilled patties, crisp lettuce, and melted cheese, all nestled
                    in a perfectly toasted bun. Paired with crispy golden fries, this hearty combo is perfect for those
                    who want more of the spicy, cheesy goodness and crispy satisfaction in every bite.
                  </p>

                  <h1 class="text-base-content jehtf leading-[1.15] fl9z1 lfitq max-md:text-3xl">
                    Mexican Grilled Cheese Burger + Coke
                  </h1>
                  <p class="text-base-content/80">
                    Satisfy your taste buds with our mouthwatering Mexican Grilled Cheese Burger a bold fusion of spicy
                    Mexican herbs, juicy grilled patty, crisp lettuce, and melted cheese, all tucked into a toasted bun.
                    This fiery delight is perfectly paired with an ice-cold Coca-Cola, making it the ultimate combo for
                    those who crave flavor, comfort, and refreshment in every bite.
                  </p>

                  <img src="https://cdn.flyonui.com/fy-assets/blocks/marketing-ui/hero/hero-37.png" class="q5ij7 c7ys3" alt="burger">
                  <div class="text-primary border-base-content/20 absolute qxfcv woq0k flex ub483 items-center justify-center rounded-full border qr9u1 bk5oo font-medium">

                  <div class="text-primary border-base-content/20 absolute fyg9a top-0 flex ub483 items-center justify-center rounded-full border qr9u1 bk5oo font-medium">

              <div class="carousel-slide dpzny n85ea wfsyj x1pg6 max-lg:justify-items-center lg:grid-cols-3" style="width: 862px;">
                <div class="flex jz3o6 x1pg6 lg:col-span-2">
                  <h1 class="text-base-content jehtf leading-[1.15] fl9z1 lfitq max-md:text-3xl">
                    Egg Patty Burger
                  </h1>
                  <p class="text-base-content/80">
                    Indulge in the perfect blend of flavors with our Egg Patty Burger, featuring a perfectly fried egg
                    layered on a juicy patty, topped with fresh lettuce, melted cheese, and all tucked into a toasted
                    bun. This simple yet satisfying burger is perfect for those who enjoy a hearty meal with a delicious
                    twist.
                  </p>
                  <div>
                    <p class="text-base-content/80 axeut font-medium">Ingridients</p>
                    <div class="flex items-center rsqkx">
                      <span class="border-base-content/20 flex girx5 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border-[0.3px]">

              <div class="carousel-slide dpzny n85ea wfsyj x1pg6 max-lg:justify-items-center lg:grid-cols-3" style="width: 862px;">
                <div class="flex jz3o6 x1pg6 lg:col-span-2">
                  <h1 class="text-base-content jehtf leading-[1.15] fl9z1 lfitq max-md:text-3xl">
                    Burger with Fries, Spices &amp; Shake Bag
                  </h1>
                  <p class="text-base-content/80">
                    Experience the ultimate indulgence with our juicy burger, served alongside crispy spiced fries that
                    are perfectly seasoned to enhance every bite. To top it off, enjoy a creamy, refreshing milkshake,
                    making this combo the perfect choice for anyone craving bold flavors and smooth sweetness all in one
                    meal.
                  </p>
                  <div>
                    <p class="text-base-content/80 axeut font-medium">Ingridients</p>
                    <div class="flex items-center rsqkx">

                  <h1 class="text-base-content jehtf leading-[1.15] fl9z1 lfitq max-md:text-3xl">
                    Burger and Sprite, K-pop
                  </h1>
                  <p class="text-base-content/80">
                    Enjoy the perfect combination of a juicy burger paired with a refreshing Sprite. Add a touch of
                    K-pop vibes to your meal for an extra burst of excitement and energy. This combo is ideal for those
                    who love a satisfying bite, a cool drink, and a splash of fun, all wrapped in one!
                  </p>

    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", function () {
      const header = document.getElementById("header")

      // Function to check and apply styles based on scroll position
      function checkScroll() {
        if (window.scrollY > 0) {
          header.classList.add("bg-base-100", "shadow-sm", "shadow-base-300/20")
        } else {
          header.classList.remove("bg-base-100", "shadow-sm", "shadow-base-300/20")
        }
      }

      // Check on page load
      checkScroll()

      // Listen for scroll events
      window.addEventListener("scroll", checkScroll)

      // Fetaure marque
      const clone = document.querySelectorAll(".clone")
      clone.forEach(item => {
        const content = item.innerHTML
        item.innerHTML = content + content + content + content
      })
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

    <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/flyonui.js"></script>
    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", function () {
      const header = document.getElementById("header")

      // Function to check and apply styles based on scroll position
      function checkScroll() {
        if (window.scrollY > 0) {
          header.classList.add("backdrop-blur-md", "shadow-sm", "shadow-base-300/20")
        } else {
          header.classList.remove("backdrop-blur-md", "shadow-sm", "shadow-base-300/20")
        }
      }

      // Check on page load
      checkScroll()

      // Listen for scroll events
      window.addEventListener("scroll", checkScroll)

      // Fetaure marque
      const clone = document.querySelectorAll(".clone")
      clone.forEach(item => {
        const content = item.innerHTML
        item.innerHTML = content + content + content + content
      })
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", function () {
      const header = document.getElementById("header")

      // Function to check and apply styles based on scroll position
      function checkScroll() {
        if (window.scrollY > 0) {
          header.classList.add("bg-base-100", "shadow-sm", "shadow-base-300/20")
        } else {
          header.classList.remove("bg-base-100", "shadow-sm", "shadow-base-300/20")
        }
      }

      // Check on page load
      checkScroll()

      // Listen for scroll events
      window.addEventListener("scroll", checkScroll)
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", function () {
      const header = document.getElementById("header")

      // Function to check and apply styles based on scroll position
      function checkScroll() {
        if (window.scrollY > 0) {
          header.classList.add("bg-base-100", "shadow-sm", "shadow-base-300/20")
        } else {
          header.classList.remove("bg-base-100", "shadow-sm", "shadow-base-300/20")
        }
      }

      // Check on page load
      checkScroll()

      // Listen for scroll events
      window.addEventListener("scroll", checkScroll)
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

  <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flatpickr/dist/flatpickr.js"></script>

    <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/flyonui.js"></script>
    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", function () {
      // Basic
      flatpickr("#vacation-date", {
        dateFormat: "F j, Y",
        weekNumbers: true
      })
      flatpickr("#flight-date", {
        dateFormat: "F j, Y",
        weekNumbers: true
      })
      flatpickr("#hotel-date", {
        dateFormat: "F j, Y to F j, Y",
        weekNumbers: true,
        mode: "range"
      })

      const header = document.getElementById("header")

      // Function to check and apply styles based on scroll position
      function checkScroll() {
        if (window.scrollY > 0) {
          header.classList.add("bg-base-100", "shadow-sm", "shadow-base-300/20")
        } else {
          header.classList.remove("bg-base-100", "shadow-sm", "shadow-base-300/20")
        }
      }

      // Check on page load
      checkScroll()

      // Listen for scroll events
      window.addEventListener("scroll", checkScroll)
    })
  </script>

    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", function () {
      const header = document.getElementById("header")

      // Function to check and apply styles based on scroll position
      function checkScroll() {
        if (window.scrollY > 0) {
          header.classList.add("bg-base-100", "shadow-sm", "shadow-base-300/20")
        } else {
          header.classList.remove("bg-base-100", "shadow-sm", "shadow-base-300/20")
        }
      }

      // Check on page load
      checkScroll()

      // Listen for scroll events
      window.addEventListener("scroll", checkScroll)
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", function () {

      // Function to check and apply styles based on scroll position
      function checkScroll() {
        if (window.scrollY > 0) {
          header.classList.remove("backdrop-blur-3xl", "bg-white/20")
          header.classList.add("bg-white")
        } else {

          header.classList.remove("bg-white")
        }
      }

      // Check on page load
      checkScroll()

      // Listen for scroll events
      window.addEventListener("scroll", checkScroll)
    })
  </script>
